- **film-action-design-coach-image-pipeline**: 用于动作任务和胜负后果已经成立，需要把招式设计、演员或替身训练、现场动作指导与影像化排成阶段交付时，输出动作分解、安全门、排练计划和拍摄接口。不用于动作场的剧情任务尚未成立；输入尚未确认动作场必要性时改用 film-action-story-task-motivation。 ([vegelate/agent-skills](https://github.com/vegelate/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Movie/film-action-design-coach-image-pipeline))
- **film-action-feature-camera-emotion-map**: 用于动作节拍已经设计并能安全重复，当前要选择景别、角度、屏幕方向和摄影机运动，使攻防因果清楚且传达角色恐惧、优势或失衡时，交付动作地理图与机位表。不用于动作尚未经过安全设计和重复排练；输入尚未成为安全可重复的动作时改用 film-action-design-coach-image-pipeline。 ([vegelate/agent-skills](https://github.com/vegelate/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Movie/film-action-feature-camera-emotion-map))
- **film-action-story-task-motivation**: 用于尚未决定动作场是否应存在、人物为何此刻必须诉诸动作、胜负会改变什么时，交付动作任务、开打理由、升级节拍与结束状态。不用于纯竞技、MV或演示项目已明确不承担剧情功能；输入已经是获准保留的动作任务时改用 film-action-design-coach-image-pipeline。 ([vegelate/agent-skills](https://github.com/vegelate/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Movie/film-action-story-task-motivation))
- **film-advisor**: Explicit-only front door for a bounded AI film-production workflow. ([Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins](https://github.com/Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ai-film-studio/skills/film-advisor) · ★2)
- **film-analysis-description-citation-context**: 用于电影分析论证尚未分清三类材料：银幕上可描述的事实、可定位的影片引述、以及访谈档案等外围资料；输出分层证据表和论证链。不用于已有完整三层证据，只需分析技巧的全片模式；输入已经有可定位的镜头证据时改用 film-analysis-part-whole-loop。 ([vegelate/agent-skills](https://github.com/vegelate/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Movie/film-analysis-description-citation-context))
- **film-analysis-part-whole-loop**: 用于已经掌握可定位的镜头证据，当前要检验某种构图、色彩、声音或剪辑技巧在全片如何重复、变化、反转并服务整体形式时，输出模式表和可修正的整体解释。不用于影片证据和外围资料尚未分层；输入尚未区分影片证据与外围资料时改用 film-analysis-description-citation-context。 ([vegelate/agent-skills](https://github.com/vegelate/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Movie/film-analysis-part-whole-loop))

- **film-assemble**: Assembles the rendered clips into one final cut — concatenates them in the edit-plan order (straight cuts), lays in the music bed, and adds subtitles (burned-in or soft) via ffmpeg, saving projects/project-name/final.mp4. - **film-assembly**: Cut the final film with ffmpeg - normalise clips to one size/fps, join with cuts or crossfades (optionally snapped to music beats), lay narration over a ducked music bed, and export for 16:9, 9:16, 1:1 or 4:5.
